import {
  closeSync, constants, copyFileSync, existsSync, fstatSync, fsyncSync, lstatSync, mkdirSync,
  openSync, readFileSync, readSync, readdirSync, renameSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, chmodSync
} from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { acquireServerLease } from './runtime-lease.js';
import { probeRuntimeOwner } from '../device/runtime-owner.js';

const EXCLUDED=['server-runtime.lock','plugins/registry.lock'];
const MAX_FILES=100000,MAX_BYTES=32*1024*1024*1024;
export interface StateFile { path:string;bytes:number;sha256:string;mode:number; }
interface Inventory {files:StateFile[];directories:string[];totalBytes:number;sourceDigest:string;}
export interface StateBackupPlan extends Inventory {
  schemaVersion:1;kind:'mcp-device-state-backup-plan';sourceRoot:string;backupRoot:string;dryRun:true;excluded:string[];

export interface StateBackupManifest extends Inventory {
  schemaVersion:1;kind:'mcp-device-state-backup';createdAt:string;sourceRoot:string;excluded:string[];manifestHash:string;

export interface LegacyMigrationPlan {
  schemaVersion:1;kind:'mcp-device-legacy-migration-plan';dryRun:boolean;deviceRoot:string;gatewayRoot:string;
  backupRoot:string;targetRoot:string;device:Inventory;gateway:Inventory;

function digest(value:unknown):string {return createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(value)).digest('hex');}
function fail(message:string):never {throw new Error(message);}
function relativePath(value:string):string {
  if(typeof value!=='string'||!value||value.includes('\\')||value.includes(':')||value.includes('\0')||
    path.posix.isAbsolute(value)||path.win32.isAbsolute(value)||value.split('/').some(x=>!x||x==='.'||x==='..'))
    fail('UNSAFE_BACKUP_PATH');
  return value;
}
function noSymlinkAncestors(input:string):string {
  const absolute=path.resolve(input),parsed=path.parse(absolute);let current=parsed.root;
  for(const segment of absolute.slice(parsed.root.length).split(path.sep).filter(Boolean)){
    current=path.join(current,segment);
    try{if(lstatSync(current).isSymbolicLink())fail('SYMLINK_STATE_PATH');}
    catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='ENOENT')break;throw error;}
  }
  return absolute;
}
function sourceRoot(input:string):string {
  const root=noSymlinkAncestors(input);
  if(root===path.parse(root).root||!existsSync(root)||!lstatSync(root).isDirectory())fail('STATE_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_REQUIRED');
  return root;
}
function absentTarget(input:string):string {
  const target=noSymlinkAncestors(input);
  if(target===path.parse(target).root||existsSync(target))fail('STATE_TARGET_MUST_NOT_EXIST');
  return target;
}
function contained(a:string,b:string):boolean {const r=path.relative(a,b);return r===''||(!r.startsWith('..'+path.sep)&&r!=='..'&&!path.isAbsolute(r));}
function separate(...roots:string[]):void {
  for(let i=0;i<roots.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<roots.length;j++)
    if(contained(roots[i],roots[j])||contained(roots[j],roots[i]))fail('STATE_ROOTS_OVERLAP');
}
async function assertStopped(root:string,ownedLease=false):Promise<void> {
  const owner=await probeRuntimeOwner({root,identity:path.join(root,'gateway-identity.json'),config:path.join(root,'gateway-config.json'),
    status:path.join(root,'gateway-status.json'),runtime:path.join(root,'runtime'),update:path.join(root,'update')});
  if(owner)fail('DEVICE_RUNTIME_ACTIVE');
  if(existsSync(path.join(root,'runtime-control.json')))fail('DEVICE_RUNTIME_STOP_NOT_PROVEN');
  const registry=path.join(root,'plugins','registry.lock');
  if(existsSync(registry))fail('PLUGIN_REGISTRY_BUSY');
  const lease=path.join(root,'server-runtime.lock');
  if(!ownedLease&&existsSync(lease)){
    let pid:number;
    try{pid=JSON.parse(readFileSync(lease,'utf8')).pid;}catch{fail('STATE_RUNTIME_LEASE_UNREADABLE');}
    if(!Number.isSafeInteger(pid!)||pid!<=0)fail('STATE_RUNTIME_LEASE_UNREADABLE');
    try{process.kill(pid!,0);fail('STATE_RUNTIME_ACTIVE');}
    catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='ESRCH')throw error;}
  }
}
function fileRecord(root:string,relative:string):StateFile {
  relativePath(relative);const full=path.join(root,...relative.split('/'));
  noSymlinkAncestors(full);
  const before=lstatSync(full);if(!before.isFile()||before.isSymbolicLink())fail('NONREGULAR_STATE_FILE');
  if(before.size>MAX_BYTES)fail('STATE_BACKUP_TOO_LARGE');
  const fd=openSync(full,constants.O_RDONLY|(constants.O_NOFOLLOW??0));
  const hasher=createHash('sha256'),buffer=Buffer.allocUnsafe(64*1024);let offset=0;
  try{
    if(!fstatSync(fd).isFile())fail('NONREGULAR_STATE_FILE');
    for(;;){const size=readSync(fd,buffer,0,buffer.length,offset);if(!size)break;hasher.update(buffer.subarray(0,size));offset+=size;}
    const after=fstatSync(fd);
    if(offset!==before.size||after.size!==before.size||after.mtimeMs!==before.mtimeMs||after.ino!==before.ino)fail('STATE_CHANGED_DURING_BACKUP');
  }finally{closeSync(fd);}
  return {path:relative,bytes:offset,sha256:hasher.digest('hex'),mode:before.mode&0o777};
}
function inventory(root:string,options:{excludeLocks?:boolean;rejectWal?:boolean}={}):Inventory {
  const files:StateFile[]=[],directories:string[]=[];let totalBytes=0;
  const names=new Set<string>();
  const visit=(directory:string,prefix='')=>{
    for(const entry of readdirSync(directory,{withFileTypes:true}).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))){
      const rel=relativePath(prefix?prefix+'/'+entry.name:entry.name);
      if(options.excludeLocks!==false&&EXCLUDED.includes(rel))continue;
      // An ancestor lease does not stop a nested Device/server or its plugin writer.
      if(options.excludeLocks!==false&&rel.includes('/')&&
        (['server-runtime.lock','runtime-control.json'].includes(entry.name)||rel.endsWith('/plugins/registry.lock')))
        fail('NESTED_RUNTIME_REQUIRES_SEPARATE_STOPPED_BACKUP');
      const key=rel.toLocaleLowerCase('en-US');if(names.has(key))fail('CASE_COLLIDING_STATE_PATH');names.add(key);
      const full=path.join(directory,entry.name),stat=lstatSync(full);
      if(stat.isSymbolicLink())fail('SYMLINK_STATE_PATH');
      if(stat.isDirectory()){directories.push(rel);visit(full,rel);continue;}
      if(!stat.isFile())fail('NONREGULAR_STATE_FILE');
      if(options.rejectWal!==false&&/(?:-wal|-journal)$/i.test(rel)&&stat.size>0)fail('SQLITE_CHECKPOINT_REQUIRED');
      if(files.length>=MAX_FILES)fail('STATE_FILE_LIMIT');
      const file=fileRecord(root,rel);files.push(file);totalBytes+=file.bytes;
      if(totalBytes>MAX_BYTES)fail('STATE_BACKUP_TOO_LARGE');
    }
  };
  visit(root);files.sort((a,b)=>a.path.localeCompare(b.path));directories.sort();
  return {files,directories,totalBytes,sourceDigest:digest({files:files.map(({mode,...file})=>file),directories,totalBytes})};
}
function flushFile(file:string):void {
  const mode=lstatSync(file).mode&0o777;chmodSync(file,mode|0o600);
  const fd=openSync(file,'r+');try{fsyncSync(fd);}finally{closeSync(fd);chmodSync(file,mode);}
}
function writeJson(file:string,value:unknown):void {writeFileSync(file,JSON.stringify(value,null,2)+'\n',{mode:0o600,flag:'wx'});flushFile(file);}
function copyInventory(from:string,to:string,items:Inventory):void {
  mkdirSync(to,{recursive:false,mode:0o700});
  for(const directory of items.directories)mkdirSync(path.join(to,...relativePath(directory).split('/')),{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
  for(const file of items.files){
    const destination=path.join(to,...relativePath(file.path).split('/'));mkdirSync(path.dirname(destination),{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
    copyFileSync(path.join(from,...file.path.split('/')),destination,constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
    chmodSync(destination,file.mode);flushFile(destination);
    if(fileRecord(to,file.path).sha256!==file.sha256)fail('STATE_CHANGED_DURING_BACKUP');
  }
}
function stageFor(target:string):string {
  mkdirSync(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true,mode:0o700});noSymlinkAncestors(path.dirname(target));
  const stage=path.join(path.dirname(target),'.'+path.basename(target)+'.'+randomUUID()+'.staging');
  mkdirSync(stage,{mode:0o700});return stage;
}
function publish(stage:string,target:string):void {absentTarget(target);renameSync(stage,target);}
async function destinationGuard<T>(target:string,operation:()=>Promise<T>):Promise<T> {
  mkdirSync(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true,mode:0o700});noSymlinkAncestors(path.dirname(target));
  const guard=path.join(path.dirname(target),'.'+path.basename(target)+'.state-operation.lock');
  let fd:number;
  try{fd=openSync(guard,'wx',0o600);}catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code==='EEXIST')fail('STATE_OPERATION_BUSY');throw error;}
  try{writeFileSync(fd,JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,token:randomUUID()}));return await operation();}
  finally{closeSync(fd);rmSync(guard,{force:true});}
}
function manifestFor(root:string,items:Inventory):StateBackupManifest {
  const base={schemaVersion:1 as const,kind:'mcp-device-state-backup' as const,createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    sourceRoot:root,...items,excluded:[...EXCLUDED]};
  return {...base,manifestHash:digest(base)};
}
function writeBundle(root:string,destination:string,items:Inventory):StateBackupManifest {
  mkdirSync(destination,{mode:0o700});
  copyInventory(root,path.join(destination,'data'),items);
  const manifest=manifestFor(root,items);writeJson(path.join(destination,'manifest.json'),manifest);
  return manifest;
}
function verifyBundle(root:string):StateBackupManifest {
  sourceRoot(root);const manifestPath=path.join(root,'manifest.json');noSymlinkAncestors(manifestPath);
  if(lstatSync(manifestPath).size>32*1024*1024)fail('BACKUP_MANIFEST_TOO_LARGE');
  const parsed=JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath,'utf8')) as StateBackupManifest;
  if(parsed.schemaVersion!==1||parsed.kind!=='mcp-device-state-backup'||!Array.isArray(parsed.files)||
    !Array.isArray(parsed.directories)||parsed.files.length>MAX_FILES)fail('INVALID_BACKUP_MANIFEST');
  const {manifestHash,...body}=parsed;if(digest(body)!==manifestHash)fail('BACKUP_MANIFEST_HASH_MISMATCH');
  for(const file of parsed.files){
    relativePath(file.path);
    if(!Number.isSafeInteger(file.bytes)||file.bytes<0||!Number.isSafeInteger(file.mode)||file.mode<0||file.mode>0o777||
      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(file.sha256))fail('INVALID_BACKUP_MANIFEST');
  }
  for(const directory of parsed.directories)relativePath(directory);
  const data=sourceRoot(path.join(root,'data')),actual=inventory(data,{excludeLocks:false,rejectWal:false});
  if(actual.sourceDigest!==parsed.sourceDigest||actual.totalBytes!==parsed.totalBytes||
    digest(actual.files.map(({mode,...file})=>file))!==digest(parsed.files.map(({mode,...file})=>file))||digest(actual.directories)!==digest(parsed.directories))fail('BACKUP_CONTENT_HASH_MISMATCH');
  const extras=readdirSync(root).filter(name=>!['data','manifest.json'].includes(name));
  if(extras.length)fail('UNMANIFESTED_BACKUP_FILE');
  return parsed;
}

export async function planStateBackup(options:{sourceRoot:string;backupRoot:string}):Promise<StateBackupPlan> {
  const root=sourceRoot(options.sourceRoot),backupRoot=absentTarget(options.backupRoot);separate(root,backupRoot);await assertStopped(root);
  return {schemaVersion:1,kind:'mcp-device-state-backup-plan',sourceRoot:root,backupRoot,dryRun:true,excluded:[...EXCLUDED],...inventory(root)};
}
export async function createStateBackup(options:{sourceRoot:string;backupRoot:string}):Promise<{backupRoot:string;manifest:StateBackupManifest}> {
  const root=sourceRoot(options.sourceRoot),backupRoot=absentTarget(options.backupRoot);separate(root,backupRoot);
  return destinationGuard(backupRoot,async()=>{
    const release=await acquireServerLease(root);let stage:string|undefined;
    try{
      await assertStopped(root,true);const items=inventory(root);stage=stageFor(backupRoot);
      const bundle=path.join(stage,'bundle');const manifest=writeBundle(root,bundle,items);
      await assertStopped(root,true);if(inventory(root).sourceDigest!==items.sourceDigest)fail('STATE_CHANGED_DURING_BACKUP');
      verifyBundle(bundle);publish(bundle,backupRoot);return {backupRoot,manifest};
    }finally{if(stage)rmSync(stage,{recursive:true,force:true});await release();}
  });
}
export async function verifyStateBackup(options:{backupRoot:string}):Promise<{valid:true;backupRoot:string;manifest:StateBackupManifest}> {
  const backupRoot=sourceRoot(options.backupRoot);return {valid:true,backupRoot,manifest:verifyBundle(backupRoot)};
}
export async function restoreStateBackup(options:{backupRoot:string;targetRoot:string}):Promise<{targetRoot:string;files:number;bytes:number;sourceDigest:string}> {
  const backupRoot=sourceRoot(options.backupRoot),targetRoot=absentTarget(options.targetRoot);separate(backupRoot,targetRoot);
  return destinationGuard(targetRoot,async()=>{
    const manifest=verifyBundle(backupRoot);const stage=stageFor(targetRoot),restored=path.join(stage,'state');
    try{
      copyInventory(path.join(backupRoot,'data'),restored,manifest);
      if(inventory(restored,{excludeLocks:false,rejectWal:false}).sourceDigest!==manifest.sourceDigest)fail('RESTORE_HASH_MISMATCH');
      verifyBundle(backupRoot);publish(restored,targetRoot);
      return {targetRoot,files:manifest.files.length,bytes:manifest.totalBytes,sourceDigest:manifest.sourceDigest};
    }finally{rmSync(stage,{recursive:true,force:true});}
  });
}
function collisionCheck(device:Inventory,gateway:Inventory):void {
  const files=new Set(device.files.map(f=>f.path.toLowerCase())),dirs=new Set(device.directories.map(d=>d.toLowerCase()));
  for(const file of gateway.files)if(files.has(file.path.toLowerCase())||dirs.has(file.path.toLowerCase()))fail('MIGRATION_PATH_COLLISION:'+file.path);
  for(const directory of gateway.directories)if(files.has(directory.toLowerCase()))fail('MIGRATION_PATH_COLLISION:'+directory);
}
export async function planLegacyMigration(options:{deviceRoot:string;gatewayRoot:string;targetRoot:string;backupRoot:string;dryRun?:boolean}):Promise<LegacyMigrationPlan> {
  const deviceRoot=sourceRoot(options.deviceRoot),gatewayRoot=sourceRoot(options.gatewayRoot),
    targetRoot=absentTarget(options.targetRoot),backupRoot=absentTarget(options.backupRoot);
  separate(deviceRoot,gatewayRoot,targetRoot,backupRoot);await assertStopped(deviceRoot);await assertStopped(gatewayRoot);
  const device=inventory(deviceRoot),gateway=inventory(gatewayRoot);collisionCheck(device,gateway);
  if(!gateway.files.some(file=>file.path==='gateway.sqlite'))fail('LEGACY_GATEWAY_DATABASE_REQUIRED');
  const mappings:LegacyMigrationPlan['mappings']=[
    ...device.files.map(file=>({source:'device' as const,path:file.path,targetPath:file.path})),
    ...gateway.files.map(file=>({source:'gateway' as const,path:file.path,targetPath:file.path}))
  ];
  const base={schemaVersion:1 as const,kind:'mcp-device-legacy-migration-plan' as const,dryRun:options.dryRun!==false,
    deviceRoot,gatewayRoot,targetRoot,backupRoot,device,gateway,mappings};
  return {...base,planHash:digest(base)};
}
export async function applyLegacyMigration(options:{plan:LegacyMigrationPlan}):Promise<{
  targetRoot:string;backupRoot:string;files:number;sourceDigest:string;rollback:{deviceBackup:string;gatewayBackup:string}
}> {
  const plan=options.plan,{planHash,...body}=plan;
  if(plan.schemaVersion!==1||plan.kind!=='mcp-device-legacy-migration-plan'||digest(body)!==planHash)fail('MIGRATION_PLAN_HASH_MISMATCH');
  const fresh=await planLegacyMigration(plan);
  if(fresh.planHash!==plan.planHash)fail('MIGRATION_PLAN_STALE');
  return destinationGuard(plan.targetRoot,async()=>destinationGuard(plan.backupRoot,async()=>{
    const releases:Array<()=>Promise<void>>=[];let backupStage:string|undefined,targetStage:string|undefined;
    try{
      for(const root of [plan.deviceRoot,plan.gatewayRoot].sort())releases.push(await acquireServerLease(root));
      await assertStopped(plan.deviceRoot,true);await assertStopped(plan.gatewayRoot,true);
      const device=inventory(plan.deviceRoot),gateway=inventory(plan.gatewayRoot);collisionCheck(device,gateway);
      if(device.sourceDigest!==plan.device.sourceDigest||gateway.sourceDigest!==plan.gateway.sourceDigest)fail('MIGRATION_PLAN_STALE');
      backupStage=stageFor(plan.backupRoot);
      const bundle=path.join(backupStage,'backup');mkdirSync(bundle,{mode:0o700});
      writeBundle(plan.deviceRoot,path.join(bundle,'device'),device);writeBundle(plan.gatewayRoot,path.join(bundle,'gateway'),gateway);
      writeJson(path.join(bundle,'migration-plan.json'),plan);
      verifyBundle(path.join(bundle,'device'));verifyBundle(path.join(bundle,'gateway'));
      if(inventory(plan.deviceRoot).sourceDigest!==device.sourceDigest||inventory(plan.gatewayRoot).sourceDigest!==gateway.sourceDigest)fail('MIGRATION_PLAN_STALE');
      publish(bundle,plan.backupRoot);
      targetStage=stageFor(plan.targetRoot);const target=path.join(targetStage,'state');
      const combined:Inventory={files:[...device.files,...gateway.files].sort((a,b)=>a.path.localeCompare(b.path)),
        directories:[...new Set([...device.directories,...gateway.directories])].sort(),
        totalBytes:device.totalBytes+gateway.totalBytes,sourceDigest:''};
      combined.sourceDigest=digest({files:combined.files.map(({mode,...file})=>file),directories:combined.directories,totalBytes:combined.totalBytes});
      mkdirSync(target,{mode:0o700});
      for(const directory of combined.directories)mkdirSync(path.join(target,...directory.split('/')),{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
      for(const [name,items] of [['device',device],['gateway',gateway]] as const)for(const file of items.files){
        const destination=path.join(target,...file.path.split('/'));mkdirSync(path.dirname(destination),{recursive:true,mode:0o700});
        copyFileSync(path.join(plan.backupRoot,name,'data',...file.path.split('/')),destination,constants.COPYFILE_EXCL);
        chmodSync(destination,file.mode);flushFile(destination);
      }
      if(inventory(target,{excludeLocks:false,rejectWal:false}).sourceDigest!==combined.sourceDigest)fail('MIGRATION_RESTORE_HASH_MISMATCH');
      publish(target,plan.targetRoot);
      return {targetRoot:plan.targetRoot,backupRoot:plan.backupRoot,files:combined.files.length,sourceDigest:combined.sourceDigest,
        rollback:{deviceBackup:path.join(plan.backupRoot,'device'),gatewayBackup:path.join(plan.backupRoot,'gateway')}};
    }finally{
      if(backupStage)rmSync(backupStage,{recursive:true,force:true});if(targetStage)rmSync(targetStage,{recursive:true,force:true});
      for(const release of releases.reverse())await release();
    }
  }));
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { existsSync, lstatSync, readFileSync, realpathSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

import { preview, boundedInt } from './util.js';
import type { KnowledgeCard } from './types.js';

// Standalone read-only CLI for the installable Palace plugin.
// Reads a live SQLite WAL snapshot safely, without invoking MCP or opening a second writer.
interface Params { command:string; positional:string[]; opts:Record<string,string|boolean> }
interface CliDetails { runtimeDir?:string|null;dataRoot?:string;pluginState?:string;runtimeActive?:boolean|null;remediation?:string;recordedError?:string }
class PalaceCliError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly code:string,message:string,readonly details:CliDetails={}){super(message);this.name='PalaceCliError';}
}
const packageRoot=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../',import.meta.url));
function packageInfo():{packageName:string;packageVersion:string;installPath:string}{
  try {const pkg=JSON.parse(readFileSync(path.join(packageRoot,'package.json'),'utf8'));return {packageName:String(pkg.name??'unknown'),packageVersion:String(pkg.version??'unknown'),installPath:packageRoot};}
  catch{return {packageName:'unknown',packageVersion:'unknown',installPath:packageRoot};}
}
function parse(argv:string[]):Params {
  const positional:string[]=[]; const opts:Record<string,string|boolean>={};
  const valued=new Set(['runtime-dir','data-root','project-id','limit','max-bytes','target','forget-owner']);
  const flags=new Set(['include-candidates','include-inactive','full','help','break-lock','abandon-pending','all']);
  for(let i=0;i<argv.length;i++){
    const part=argv[i];
    if(!part.startsWith('--')){positional.push(part);continue;}
    const eq=part.indexOf('=');const key=part.slice(2,eq<0?undefined:eq);
    if(flags.has(key)&&eq<0){opts[key]=true;continue;}
    if(!valued.has(key))throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','Unknown option: '+key);
    const value=eq<0?argv[++i]:part.slice(eq+1);
    if(!value||value.startsWith('--'))throw new PalaceCliError('MISSING_OPTION','Missing option: '+key);
    opts[key]=value;
  }
  return { command: positional.shift()??'status', positional, opts };
}
function checkedString(value:unknown,max=4096):string {
  if(typeof value!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(value,'utf8')>max)throw new PalaceCliError('INVALID_ARGUMENT','Invalid string argument');
  return value;
}
async function runtimeIsActive(runtime:string):Promise<boolean|null> {
  if(!existsSync(path.join(runtime,'server-runtime.lock'))&&existsSync(path.join(runtime,'runtime-control.json')))return null;
  const inspection=await inspectRuntimeRoot(runtime);
  return inspection.leaseInspectable?inspection.activeLease:null;
}
/** Recognize the generic host's existing private-data layout even through an
 * alias. An explicit directory is not permission to ignore a known disabled
 * runtime. Separately copied archives have no binding and remain read-only. */
function managedRuntime(dataRoot:string):string|null{
  let canonical:string;try{canonical=realpathSync(dataRoot);}catch{return null;}
  const palace=path.dirname(canonical),plugins=path.dirname(palace),runtime=path.dirname(plugins);
  const names=[path.basename(plugins),path.basename(palace),path.basename(canonical)].map(name=>process.platform==='win32'?name.toLowerCase():name);
  if(names.join('/')!=='plugins/palace/data')return null;
  return existsSync(path.join(runtime,'plugins','registry.json'))?runtime:null;
}
function lifecycle(runtime:string,explicitDataRoot:boolean,pkg:{packageName:string;installPath:string}):{pluginState:string;available:boolean;code?:string;remediation?:string;recordedError?:string}{
  if(explicitDataRoot)return {pluginState:'unknown',available:true};
  const registry=path.join(runtime,'plugins','registry.json');
  if(!existsSync(registry))return {pluginState:'unregistered',available:true};
  let record:any;
  try{record=JSON.parse(readFileSync(registry,'utf8'))?.plugins?.palace;}
  catch{throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_REGISTRY_INVALID','Palace plugin registry is unreadable',{runtimeDir:runtime,remediation:'Inspect or restore plugins/registry.json before using Palace.'});}
  if(!record)return {pluginState:'not-installed',available:false,code:'PALACE_NOT_INSTALLED',remediation:`Install or restore Palace for runtime ${runtime}.`};
  if(existsSync(path.join(runtime,'plugins','palace','upgrade-intent.json')))
    return {pluginState:'upgrade-in-progress',available:false,code:'PALACE_UPGRADE_IN_PROGRESS',remediation:`Finish or recover the Palace upgrade with mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" list before using Palace.`};
  if(!record.installed||record.state==='uninstalled'){
    const remediation=pkg.packageName==='@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device'
      ? `Run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" install palace --bundled --enable.`
      : `Register the standalone package with an existing Device host: mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" install "${path.join(pkg.installPath,'palace.plugin.json')}" --enable. A standalone host without mcp-device has no mutation/re-registration path.`;
    return {pluginState:'uninstalled',available:false,code:'PALACE_UNINSTALLED',remediation};
  }
  const stopWarning='An agent must not stop the runtime itself; report this prerequisite to the operator.';
  if(record.bundleUpgradeRequired)return {pluginState:'upgrade-required',available:false,code:'PALACE_UPGRADE_REQUIRED',remediation:`Stop the runtime, then run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" upgrade palace --bundled. ${stopWarning}`};
  if(!record.enabled||record.state==='disabled')return {pluginState:'disabled',available:false,code:'PALACE_DISABLED',remediation:`Run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" enable palace.`};
  if(record.state==='faulted'){
    const recordedError=String(record.error??'PLUGIN_FAULT: no recorded diagnostic');
    let missingEntry=false;
    const entry=record.manifest?.entry;
    if(typeof entry==='string'&&path.isAbsolute(entry)){
      try{missingEntry=!statSync(entry).isFile();}
      catch(error){missingEntry=['ENOENT','ENOTDIR'].includes(String((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code));}
    }
    let recovery=`Inspect the recorded fault and its inputs, then run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" enable palace.`;
    if(missingEntry){
      recovery=record.installationSource==='distribution'
        ?`Stop the runtime, then run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" upgrade palace --bundled. ${stopWarning}`
        :`Stop the runtime, restore the manually installed package, then run mcp-device plugins --runtime-dir "${runtime}" upgrade "/path/to/reviewed/palace.plugin.json" using its reviewed manifest. Do not substitute the bundled distribution. ${stopWarning}`;
    }
    return {pluginState:'faulted',available:false,code:'PALACE_FAULTED',recordedError,remediation:`Recorded fault: ${recordedError}. ${recovery}`};
  }
  return {pluginState:String(record.state??'installed'),available:true};
}
function emit(value:Record<string,unknown>,maxBytes:number):void {
  const bytes=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)+'\n');
  if(bytes.length>maxBytes)throw new PalaceCliError('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL',`${bytes.length} > ${maxBytes}`);
  process.stdout.write(bytes);
}
function emitSkillLifecycle(value:Record<string,unknown>,maxBytes:number):void {
  const bytes=Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)+'\n');if(bytes.length<=maxBytes){process.stdout.write(bytes);return;}
  const rows=Array.isArray(value.result)?value.result as Record<string,unknown>[]:[];
  emit({ok:value.ok,skill:value.skill,home:value.home,optedOut:value.optedOut,stillProvidedBy:value.stillProvidedBy,
    ownerProblemCount:Array.isArray(value.ownerProblems)?value.ownerProblems.length:0,pendingRecovery:Boolean(value.pendingRecovery),truncated:true,
    result:rows.map(row=>({target:row.target,state:row.state,code:row.code,remediation:typeof row.remediation==='string'?row.remediation.slice(0,512):row.remediation}))},maxBytes);
}
function unavailableBase(code:string,message:string,base:Record<string,unknown>,details:CliDetails):Record<string,unknown>{
  return {...base,ok:false,code,error:message,...details};
}
function schema(db:DatabaseSync,details:CliDetails):void {
  try{
    const hasMeta=Boolean((db.prepare("select 1 as present from sqlite_master where type='table' and name='meta'").get() as {present?:number}|undefined)?.present);
    if(!hasMeta)throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED','Palace schema metadata is missing',details);
    const value=String((db.prepare('select value from meta where key=?').get('schema') as {value?:string}|undefined)?.value??'');
    if(value!=='1')throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED','Unsupported Palace schema: '+(value||'missing'),details);
  }catch(error){if(error instanceof PalaceCliError)throw error;throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED','Palace schema cannot be read',details);}
}
async function main():Promise<void> {
  const {command,positional,opts}=parse(process.argv.slice(2));
  if(opts.help){
    process.stdout.write('palace [--data-root DIR|--runtime-dir DIR] status|search QUERY|read ID|skills|skill status|install|repair|remove|adopt [--project-id ID] [--limit N] [--max-bytes N] [--include-candidates] [--full]\n');
    return;
  }
  if(!['status','search','read','skills','skill'].includes(command))throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_COMMAND','Unknown Palace command');
  if(opts['data-root']!==undefined&&opts['runtime-dir']!==undefined)throw new PalaceCliError('CONFLICTING_OPTIONS','Use either --data-root or --runtime-dir, not both');
  if(['status','skills'].includes(command)&&positional.length)throw new PalaceCliError('UNEXPECTED_ARGUMENT','This command accepts no positional arguments');
  if(command==='read'&&positional.length!==1)throw new PalaceCliError('CARD_ID_REQUIRED','Exactly one card id is required');
  if(command==='search'&&!positional.join(' ').trim())throw new PalaceCliError('QUERY_REQUIRED','A query is required');
  if(command==='skill'&&positional.length>1)throw new PalaceCliError('UNEXPECTED_ARGUMENT','Use one Skill lifecycle action');
  const skillAction=command==='skill'?(positional[0]??'status'):null;
  if((opts['break-lock']||opts['abandon-pending'])&&skillAction!=='repair')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','Recovery options are only valid for skill repair');
  if(opts.all&&skillAction!=='remove')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','--all is only valid for skill remove');
  if(opts.target!==undefined&&skillAction!=='adopt')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','--target is only valid for skill adopt');
  if(opts['forget-owner']!==undefined&&skillAction!=='repair')throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_OPTION','--forget-owner is only valid for skill repair');
  const maxBytes=boundedInt(opts['max-bytes']===undefined?undefined:Number(opts['max-bytes']),16384,512,65536);
  if(command==='skill'){
    const action=positional[0]??'status';let result:Record<string,unknown>;
    if(action==='status'||action==='doctor')result=await palaceSkillStatus({packageRoot});
    else if(action==='install')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,install:true,repair:true});
    else if(action==='repair')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,repair:true,breakLock:opts['break-lock']===true,abandonPending:opts['abandon-pending']===true,forgetOwner:typeof opts['forget-owner']==='string'?opts['forget-owner']:undefined});
    else if(action==='prune')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,prune:true});
    else if(action==='remove')result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,removeCurrentOwner:true,removeAll:opts.all===true});
    else if(action==='adopt'){
      if(opts.target!=='agents'&&opts.target!=='pi')throw new PalaceCliError('SKILL_TARGET_REQUIRED','Explicitly select --target agents or --target pi. The prior target is retained, not deleted.');
      result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({packageRoot,force:true,install:true,repair:true,adoptTarget:opts.target});
    }else throw new PalaceCliError('SKILL_ACTION_UNSUPPORTED','Use palace skill status, install, repair, remove or adopt');
    emitSkillLifecycle(result,maxBytes);if(result.ok===false)process.exitCode=1;return;
  }
  const pkg=packageInfo();
  const skill=palaceSkillQuickProbe({packageRoot},{name:pkg.packageName,version:pkg.packageVersion});
  const skillState=skill.state;
  const explicitDataRoot=opts['data-root']!==undefined||(opts['runtime-dir']===undefined&&process.env.PALACE_DATA_ROOT!==undefined);
  const selectedRuntime=explicitDataRoot?'':await resolveRuntimeRoot(opts['runtime-dir']===undefined?undefined:String(opts['runtime-dir']));
  const dataRoot=path.resolve(String(explicitDataRoot?(opts['data-root']??process.env.PALACE_DATA_ROOT):path.join(selectedRuntime,'plugins','palace','data')));
  const inferred=explicitDataRoot?managedRuntime(dataRoot):null,runtime=inferred??selectedRuntime;
  const archive=explicitDataRoot&&inferred===null,runtimeBinding=archive?'unmanaged-archive':inferred?'managed-path':'explicit-runtime';
  const filename=path.join(dataRoot,'palace.sqlite');
  if(existsSync(filename)&&lstatSync(filename).isSymbolicLink())throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_MANAGED_PATH_UNSAFE','Palace database must be an ordinary file, not a symbolic link',{runtimeDir:archive?null:runtime,dataRoot,remediation:'Use the real Palace data directory or a reviewed archive copy.'});
  const active=archive?null:await runtimeIsActive(runtime),life=lifecycle(runtime,archive,pkg);
  const writePath=archive||active!==false?null:`mcp-device knowledge --runtime-dir "${runtime}"`;
  const mutationPolicy=archive?'runtime-binding-unknown':active===true?'stopped-runtime-only':active===null?'owner-state-unknown':'exclusive-lease-required';
  const base={plugin:'palace',mode:'read-only',runtimeDir:archive?null:runtime,runtimeBinding,dataRoot,pluginState:life.pluginState,runtimeActive:active,leaseVerification:archive?null:'pid-namespace',writePath,mutationPolicy,skillState,skill,...pkg};
  const details={runtimeDir:archive?null:runtime,dataRoot,pluginState:life.pluginState,runtimeActive:active,remediation:life.remediation,recordedError:life.recordedError};
  if(!life.available){
    const failure=unavailableBase(life.code??'PALACE_UNAVAILABLE','Palace is not callable for this runtime.',base,details);
    if(command==='status'){emit(failure,maxBytes);process.exitCode=1;return;}
    throw new PalaceCliError(life.code??'PALACE_UNAVAILABLE','Palace is not callable for this runtime.',details);
  }
  if(!existsSync(filename)){
    const failure=unavailableBase('PALACE_NOT_INSTALLED','Palace database is not installed.',base,{...details,remediation:life.remediation??`Start or install Palace for runtime ${runtime}.`});
    if(command==='status'){emit(failure,maxBytes);process.exitCode=1;return;}
    throw new PalaceCliError('PALACE_NOT_INSTALLED','Palace database is not installed',{...details,remediation:String(failure.remediation)});
  }
  const db=new DatabaseSync(filename,{readOnly:true});
  try {
    db.exec('PRAGMA busy_timeout=3000');
    // Establish one WAL snapshot for schema, counts and selected rows in this invocation.
    db.exec('BEGIN');
    schema(db,details);
    const limit=boundedInt(opts.limit===undefined?undefined:Number(opts.limit),10,1,50);
    const projectId=opts['project-id']===undefined?undefined:checkedString(opts['project-id'],512);
    const includeInactive=opts['include-inactive']===true;
    const includeCandidates=opts['include-candidates']===true;
    const full=opts.full===true;
    let response:Record<string,unknown>;
    if(command==='status'){
      const counts=(db.prepare('select kind,status,count(*) as count from cards group by kind,status').all() as Record<string,unknown>[]);
      const totals=(db.prepare('select (select count(*) from cards) cards,(select count(*) from evidence) evidence,(select count(*) from versions) versions').get() as Record<string,unknown>);
      response={...base,ok:true,totals,counts};
    }else if(command==='search'){
      const query=checkedString(positional.join(' '));if(!query.trim())throw new PalaceCliError('QUERY_REQUIRED','A search query is required',details);
      const selected=searchPalaceCards(db,'',{query,projectId,includeInactive,limit:0,resultLimit:limit});
      const result=selected.items.map(x=>full?x:preview(x));
      response={...base,ok:true,query,items:result,returned:result.length,candidates:selected.candidates,note:'Snapshot results; evidence bytes not reverified by read-only CLI'};
    }else if(command==='skills'){
      const rows=queryPalaceCards(db,'',{projectId,includeInactive:includeCandidates||includeInactive,kind:'procedure',status:includeCandidates?undefined:'validated',limit:Math.min(200,Math.max(limit+1,limit*4))});
      const visible=(includeCandidates?rows.filter(x=>x.status==='validated'||x.status==='hypothesis'):rows).slice(0,limit);
      const result=visible.map(x=>full?x:preview(x));
      const activationPolicy=String((db.prepare('select value from meta where key=?').get('activationPolicy') as {value?:string}|undefined)?.value??'candidate_only');
      response={...base,ok:true,items:result,returned:result.length,candidates:rows.filter(x=>x.status==='validated'||x.status==='hypothesis').length,activationPolicy,note:'Validated procedures only unless --include-candidates is explicit; stale/superseded/withdrawn procedures remain excluded'};
    }else if(command==='read'){
      const id=checkedString(positional[0],512);if(!id||positional.length!==1)throw new PalaceCliError('CARD_ID_REQUIRED','Exactly one card id is required',details);
      const row=db.prepare('select json,kind from cards where id=?').get(id) as {json:string;kind:string}|undefined;
      if(!row)throw new PalaceCliError('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND','Knowledge card was not found',details);
      const card=JSON.parse(row.json) as KnowledgeCard;
      if(card.scope.visibility==='project'&&(!projectId||projectId!==card.scope.projectId))throw new PalaceCliError('SCOPE_REQUIRED','Matching --project-id is required',details);
      if(!includeInactive&&['stale','superseded','withdrawn'].includes(card.status))throw new PalaceCliError('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_CURRENT','Knowledge card is inactive',details);
      response={...base,ok:true,kind:row.kind,card,evidenceVerifiedNow:false,note:'Source integrity must be verified separately before treating this card as an established fact.'};
    }else throw new PalaceCliError('UNKNOWN_COMMAND','Unknown Palace command: '+command,details);
    emit(response,maxBytes);
  } finally {db.close();}
}
try{await main();}catch(error){
  const failure=error as Error&{code?:string;details?:CliDetails};
  const text=String(failure.message??failure),rawCode=failure.code??(failure instanceof PalaceCliError?failure.code:text.split(':')[0]);
  const busy=/SQLITE_BUSY|SQLITE_LOCKED|database is locked/i.test(String(rawCode)+' '+text);
  const code=busy?'PALACE_BUSY':rawCode;
  process.stderr.write(JSON.stringify({ok:false,code,error:text,...(busy?{remediation:'Retry after the active Palace writer completes its current transaction.'}:{}),...(failure instanceof PalaceCliError?failure.details:{})})+'\n');
  process.exitCode=1;
}

import { closeSync, fsyncSync, mkdirSync, openSync, readFileSync, existsSync, writeFileSync, renameSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename, join, resolve, relative, sep, isAbsolute } from 'node:path';
import { LogPalace } from '../../log-palace/store.js';
import type { KnowledgeCard, KnowledgeEvidence, KnowledgePreview, PalaceOptions, PalaceProvider, InvokeContext, KnowledgeScope } from './types.js';
import { PalaceStore, type EvidenceLocation } from './store.js';
import { SourceCatalog, extractDocument, extractObservation, gitSnapshot, readDocument } from './sources.js';
import { abort, assertLive, boundedInt, canonical, cardHash, clip, hash, object, preview, scopeOf, text } from './util.js';
export type * from './types.js';
export { cardHash } from './util.js';

export const PALACE_CAPABILITIES = ['knowledge.search','knowledge.list','knowledge.read','knowledge.stat','knowledge.import',
  'knowledge.ingest','knowledge.correct','knowledge.learn','knowledge.skills'] as const;
type Input = Record<string,unknown>;
type SourceObservation = Record<string,unknown>;

class Provider implements PalaceProvider {
  private store?:PalaceStore;
  private logs?:LogPalace;
  private timer?:ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;
  private tail:Promise<unknown>=Promise.resolve();
  private catalog:SourceCatalog;
  private stopping=false;
  private readonly indexBudget:Input;
  private readonly indexInterval:number;
  constructor(private readonly options:PalaceOptions) {
    if(!options.deviceId||options.deviceId.length>256)throw new Error('DEVICE_ID_REQUIRED');
    this.catalog=new SourceCatalog(options);
    this.indexBudget=object(options.indexBudget??{});this.indexInterval=boundedInt(this.indexBudget.intervalMs,30000,1000,3600000);
  }
  async start():Promise<void> {
    if(this.store)return;
    this.stopping=false;mkdirSync(this.options.dataRoot,{recursive:true});
    this.store=new PalaceStore(this.options.dataRoot,this.options.deviceId);
    this.store.setMeta('activationPolicy',this.options.validationRunner?'configured':'candidate_only');
    try {this.logs=new LogPalace(join(this.options.dataRoot,'source-index'));}
    catch(error){this.store.close();this.store=undefined;throw error;}
    for(const path of this.catalog.logRoots()) {
      if(/\.(md|markdown)$/i.test(path))continue;
      try {this.logs.add(path);}catch(error){this.store.setMeta('lastDiscoveryError',String(error).slice(0,512));}
    }
    if(this.options.autoIndex!==false) {
      // Startup establishes ownership and the writer first; initial indexing is
      // queued after start returns so a large corpus cannot consume the plugin
      // driver's init deadline. All writes still serialize through this provider.
      setImmediate(()=>{if(!this.stopping)void this.invoke('knowledge.ingest',this.indexBudget).catch(error=>{
        if(this.store)this.store.setMeta('lastIndexError',String(error).slice(0,512));
      });});
      this.timer=setInterval(()=>{void this.invoke('knowledge.ingest',this.indexBudget).catch(error=>{
        if(this.store)this.store.setMeta('lastIndexError',String(error).slice(0,512));
      });},this.indexInterval);
      this.timer.unref();
    }
  }
  async stop():Promise<void> {
    this.stopping=true;if(this.timer)clearInterval(this.timer);this.timer=undefined;
    await this.tail.catch(()=>{});
    this.logs?.close();this.logs=undefined;this.store?.close();this.store=undefined;
  }
  invoke(capability:string,input:unknown,context:InvokeContext={}):Promise<unknown> {
    if(this.stopping||!this.store||!this.logs)return Promise.reject(new Error('PLUGIN_NOT_STARTED'));
    const args=object(input);
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(args),'utf8')>256*1024)return Promise.reject(new Error('INPUT_TOO_LARGE'));
    const job=this.tail.then(async()=>{abort(context.signal);return this.dispatch(capability,args,context);});
    this.tail=job.catch(()=>{});return job;
  }
  private get db():PalaceStore {if(!this.store)throw new Error('PLUGIN_NOT_STARTED');return this.store;}
  private get p1():LogPalace {if(!this.logs)throw new Error('PLUGIN_NOT_STARTED');return this.logs;}
  private id(input:Input):string {
    const ref=text(input.ref??input.id,512);if(!ref)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_REF_REQUIRED');return ref;
  }
  private card(id:string):KnowledgeCard {const card=this.db.get(id);if(!card)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND');return card;}
  private newCard(id:string,summary:string,body:string,status:KnowledgeCard['status'],scope:KnowledgeScope,
    evidence:KnowledgeEvidence[],derivedFrom:string[]=[]):KnowledgeCard {
    const now=Date.now(),card:KnowledgeCard={id,version:1,contentHash:'',summary:clip(summary,256),body:clip(body,16384),status,
      originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,servingDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,scope,evidence,
      derivedFrom:[...new Set(derivedFrom)],provenance:[this.options.deviceId],updatedAt:now,lastVerifiedAt:now};
    card.contentHash=cardHash(card);return card;
  }
  private managedEvidence(value:unknown,kind:KnowledgeEvidence['kind']):KnowledgeEvidence {
    const bytes=Buffer.from(canonical(value)+'\n'),recordHash=hash(bytes);
    if(bytes.length>128*1024)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_TOO_LARGE');
    const relativePath=join('evidence',recordHash+'.json'),path=join(this.options.dataRoot,relativePath);
    const evidenceDir=join(this.options.dataRoot,'evidence');mkdirSync(evidenceDir,{recursive:true});
    if(!existsSync(path)){
      const tmp=path+'.tmp-'+process.pid+'-'+Date.now(),fd=openSync(tmp,'wx',0o600);
      try{writeFileSync(fd,bytes);fsyncSync(fd);}finally{closeSync(fd);}
      renameSync(tmp,path);
      if(process.platform!=='win32'){const dirfd=openSync(evidenceDir,'r');try{fsyncSync(dirfd);}finally{closeSync(dirfd);}}
    }
    const evidence:KnowledgeEvidence={id:'ev:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':managed:'+recordHash),
      originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,sourceId:'managed:'+recordHash,sourceRef:'managed:'+recordHash,recordHash,
      kind,sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:Date.now()};
    this.db.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'managed',path:relativePath,bytes:bytes.length});return evidence;
  }
  private evidenceRead(location:EvidenceLocation,maxBytes=4096):Record<string,unknown> {
    if(location.type==='remote')return {id:location.evidence.id,available:false,reason:'REMOTE_EVIDENCE_REFERENCE',
      originDeviceId:location.evidence.originDeviceId,sourceRef:location.evidence.sourceRef};
    if(location.type==='p1'){
      const read=this.p1.read(location.sourceRef??location.evidence.sourceRef,Math.min(65536,Math.max(128,maxBytes)));
      return {id:location.evidence.id,available:true,verifiedBytes:true,recordHash:location.evidence.recordHash,...read};
    }
    const path=location.type==='managed'?resolve(this.options.dataRoot,location.path??''):location.path??'';
    if(location.type==='managed'){
      const rel=relative(resolve(this.options.dataRoot),path);
      if(rel.startsWith('..'+sep)||rel==='..')throw new Error('EVIDENCE_PATH_INVALID');
    }
    const bytes=readFileSync(path);if(bytes.length>256*1024)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_TOO_LARGE');
    if(hash(bytes)!==location.evidence.recordHash)throw new Error('SOURCE_CHANGED');
    const decoded=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(bytes);
    return {id:location.evidence.id,available:true,verifiedBytes:true,recordHash:location.evidence.recordHash,
      text:clip(decoded,maxBytes),truncated:bytes.length>maxBytes};
  }
  private refresh(card:KnowledgeCard):KnowledgeCard {
    for(const evidence of card.evidence){
      const location=this.db.evidence(evidence.id);if(!location||location.type==='remote')continue;
      try {this.evidenceRead(location,128);}
      catch {this.db.markUnavailable(evidence.id);}
    }
    return this.card(card.id);
  }
  private checkScope(card:KnowledgeCard,input:Input):void {
    const projectId=text(input.projectId,512);
    if(projectId && card.scope.visibility==='project' && card.scope.projectId!==projectId)throw new Error('KNOWLEDGE_SCOPE_MISMATCH');
  }
  private async dispatch(capability:string,input:Input,context:InvokeContext):Promise<unknown> {
    switch(capability){
      case 'knowledge.search':return this.search(input,false);
      case 'knowledge.list':return this.search(input,true);
      case 'knowledge.stat':{let card=this.card(this.id(input));this.checkScope(card,input);card=this.refresh(card);return this.db.stat(card.id);}
      case 'knowledge.read':return this.read(input);
      case 'knowledge.import':return this.import(input);
      case 'knowledge.ingest':return this.ingest(input,context.signal);
      case 'knowledge.correct':return this.correct(input);
      case 'knowledge.learn':return this.learn(input,context.signal);
      case 'knowledge.skills':return this.skills(input,context.signal);
      default:throw new Error('CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE');
    }
  }
  private search(input:Input,list:boolean):unknown {
    const query=list?'':text(input.query,2048);
    if(!list&&!query.trim())throw new Error('QUERY_REQUIRED');
    const limit=boundedInt(input.limit,8,1,50),maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,8192,512,65536);
    const common={query,projectId:text(input.projectId,512)||undefined,
      includeInactive:input.includeInactive===true,kind:text(input.kind,64)||undefined,status:text(input.status,64)||undefined,
      cursor:list?text(input.cursor,512)||undefined:undefined};
    const rows=list?this.db.query({...common,limit:limit+1}):this.db.search({...common,limit:0,query,resultLimit:limit,lookahead:true}).items;
    const items:KnowledgePreview[]=[];
    let truncated=rows.length>limit;
    for(const card of rows.slice(0,limit)){
      const item=preview(card),proposal={items:[...items,item],total:rows.length,truncated,nextCursor:list?card.id:null};
      if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(proposal),'utf8')>maxBytes){truncated=true;break;}
      items.push(item);
    }
    const response={items,total:rows.length,truncated,nextCursor:list&&truncated&&items.length?items[items.length-1].id:null};
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes)throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
    return response;
  }
  private read(input:Input):unknown {
    const maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,65536,1024,256*1024);
    let card=this.card(this.id(input));this.checkScope(card,input);card=this.refresh(card);
    const response:{card:KnowledgeCard;evidence?:Record<string,unknown>[]}={card};
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes)throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
    if(input.evidence===true){
      response.evidence=[];
      for(const evidence of card.evidence.slice(0,32)){
        const location=this.db.evidence(evidence.id);
        let item:Record<string,unknown>;
        try {item=location?this.evidenceRead(location,Math.min(4096,maxBytes)): {id:evidence.id,available:false,reason:'REMOTE_EVIDENCE_REFERENCE'};}
        catch(error){item={id:evidence.id,available:false,reason:String(error).slice(0,160)};}
        const candidate={...response,evidence:[...response.evidence,item]};
        if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(candidate),'utf8')>maxBytes)break;
        response.evidence.push(item);
      }
    }
    return response;
  }
  private import(input:Input):unknown {
    const raw=object(input.card);
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(raw),'utf8')>65536)throw new Error('CARD_TOO_LARGE');
    const id=text(raw.id,256),origin=text(raw.originDeviceId,256);
    if(!id||!origin||!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.version)||Number(raw.version)<1)throw new Error('INVALID_CARD');
    const evidence=raw.evidence;
    if(!Array.isArray(evidence)||!evidence.length||evidence.length>32)throw new Error('INVALID_EVIDENCE');
    const ids=new Set<string>();
    for(const value of evidence){
      const e=object(value);
      if(!text(e.id,256)||!text(e.originDeviceId,256)||!text(e.sourceId,512)||!text(e.sourceRef,512)||
        !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(text(e.recordHash,64))||!['source_trace','tool_receipt','postcondition'].includes(String(e.kind)))
        throw new Error('INVALID_EVIDENCE');
      if(typeof e.sourceAvailable!=='boolean'||!Number.isFinite(e.observedAt))throw new Error('INVALID_EVIDENCE');
      if(ids.has(String(e.id)))throw new Error('DUPLICATE_EVIDENCE');ids.add(String(e.id));
    }
    if(!Array.isArray(raw.derivedFrom)||raw.derivedFrom.length>32||raw.derivedFrom.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||x.length>256))
      throw new Error('INVALID_DERIVATION');
    if(!Array.isArray(raw.provenance)||raw.provenance.length>64||raw.provenance.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||x.length>256))
      throw new Error('INVALID_PROVENANCE');
    if(!['hypothesis','observed','validated','stale','superseded','withdrawn'].includes(String(raw.status)))throw new Error('INVALID_STATUS');
    if(!Number.isFinite(raw.updatedAt)||Number(raw.updatedAt)<0||
      !(raw.lastVerifiedAt===null||Number.isFinite(raw.lastVerifiedAt)))throw new Error('INVALID_CARD_TIME');
    const card={...raw,id,originDeviceId:origin,summary:text(raw.summary,256),body:text(raw.body,32768),
      scope:scopeOf(raw.scope),servingDeviceId:this.options.deviceId} as unknown as KnowledgeCard;
    if(!card.summary||cardHash(card)!==card.contentHash)throw new Error('CARD_HASH_MISMATCH');
    if(card.scope.visibility==='local'&&origin!==this.options.deviceId)throw new Error('LOCAL_ONLY_KNOWLEDGE');
    const existing=this.db.get(id);
    if(origin===this.options.deviceId&&(!existing||card.version>existing.version))throw new Error('ORIGIN_WRITE_FORBIDDEN');
    return this.db.importCard(card);
  }
  private async ingest(input:Input,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<unknown> {
    const maxRecords=boundedInt(input.maxRecords,64,1,256),maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,2*1024*1024,1024,16*1024*1024);
    const maxFiles=boundedInt(input.maxFiles,32,1,128);abort(signal);
    for(const path of this.catalog.logRoots())if(!/\.(md|markdown)$/i.test(path))try{this.p1.add(path);}catch{}
    const index=this.p1.index({maxFiles,maxBytes});
    let remaining=maxBytes,created=0,skipped=0;
    const page=this.p1.observations({cursor:this.db.getMeta('observationCursor')||undefined,limit:maxRecords,maxBytes:1024*1024});
    for(const row of page.items as SourceObservation[]){
      abort(signal);
      const ref=String(row.ref),recordBytes=Number(row.bytes),sourcePath=String(row.source_path);
      const evidenceId='ev:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':p1:'+ref+':'+String(row.hash));
      if(!row.source_available){this.db.markUnavailable(evidenceId);skipped++;continue;}
      if(recordBytes>remaining){skipped++;continue;}
      remaining-=recordBytes;
      const id='k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':observation:'+evidenceId);
      const existing=this.db.get(id);if(existing&&existing.status!=='stale'){skipped++;continue;}
      try{
        const read=this.p1.read(ref,8192);
        const extracted=extractObservation(read.text,String(row.kind),String(row.tool??''));
        if(!extracted){skipped++;continue;}
        const evidence:KnowledgeEvidence={id:evidenceId,originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,sourceId:'source:'+hash(sourcePath),
          sourceRef:ref,recordHash:String(row.hash),kind:'source_trace',sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:Date.now()};
        const scope=this.catalog.scope(typeof row.file_path==='string'&&isAbsolute(row.file_path)?row.file_path:sourcePath);
        const normalized=(body:string)=>body.normalize('NFKC').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().toLowerCase();
        const similarityKey=hash(canonical({scope,status:extracted.status,body:normalized(extracted.body)}));
        const similar=this.db.similar(similarityKey);
        if(similar&&(similar.status==='withdrawn'||similar.status==='superseded'||normalized(similar.body)!==normalized(extracted.body))){skipped++;continue;}
        if(similar&&similar.evidence.some(e=>e.id===evidence.id&&e.sourceAvailable)&&similar.status!=='stale'){skipped++;continue;}
        if(similar&&similar.evidence.length>=32){skipped++;continue;}
        const saved=this.db.atomic(()=>{
          this.db.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'p1',sourceRef:ref,bytes:recordBytes});
          const base=similar??existing;
          const allEvidence=[...(base?.evidence??[]).filter(e=>e.sourceAvailable&&e.id!==evidence.id),evidence];
          const fresh=this.newCard(base?.id??id,extracted.summary,extracted.body,extracted.status,scope,allEvidence);
          const card=base?this.db.nextVersion(base,{status:fresh.status,evidence:allEvidence,lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()}):fresh;
          this.db.put(card);this.db.rememberSimilarity(similarityKey,card.id);return card;
        });created++;
        if(saved.evidence.length>=2){
          const fix=saved.body.match(/(?:fix|solution|resolved by)\s*:\s*([^\n]{8,1024})/i)?.[1];
          if(fix)await this.learn({key:'automatic:'+saved.id,summary:clip('Procedure candidate: '+fix,256),steps:[fix],evidenceRefs:[saved.id],autoActivate:false},signal);
        }
      }catch{skipped++;}
    }
    this.db.setMeta('observationCursor',page.nextCursor??'');
    for(const source of this.catalog.documents(Math.min(32,maxFiles))){
      abort(signal);
      try{
        const data=readDocument(source.path);if(data.bytes.length>remaining)continue;remaining-=data.bytes.length;
        const old=this.db.document(source.path);if(old?.hash===data.hash)continue;
        const extracted=extractDocument(data.text),id=old?.cardId??'k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':document:'+source.path);
        const evidence:KnowledgeEvidence={id:'ev:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':file:'+source.path+':'+data.hash),
          originDeviceId:this.options.deviceId,sourceId:'source:'+hash(source.path),sourceRef:'file:'+hash(source.path)+':'+data.hash,
          recordHash:data.hash,kind:'source_trace',sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:Date.now()};
        this.db.atomic(()=>{
          this.db.saveEvidence({evidence,type:'file',path:source.path,bytes:data.bytes.length});
          const current=this.db.get(id),fresh=this.newCard(id,extracted.summary,extracted.body,'observed',source.scope,[evidence]);
          const card=current?this.db.nextVersion(current,{summary:fresh.summary,body:fresh.body,evidence:[evidence],status:'observed',lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()}):fresh;
          this.db.put(card,source.kind);
          if(current)this.db.invalidateDependents(id);
          this.db.setDocument(source.path,data.hash,id);
        });created++;
      }catch{skipped++;}
    }
    for(const root of this.catalog.projects.slice(0,4)){
      abort(signal);const snapshot=await gitSnapshot(root);if(!snapshot||Buffer.byteLength(snapshot)>remaining)continue;
      remaining-=Buffer.byteLength(snapshot);
      const key='git:'+hash(root),revision=hash(snapshot);
      if(this.db.getMeta(key)===revision)continue;
      const evidence=this.managedEvidence({type:'git-history',project:root,output:snapshot},'source_trace');
      const id='k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':'+key),old=this.db.get(id);
      const fresh=this.newCard(id,'Recent Git history: '+basename(root),snapshot,'observed',this.catalog.scope(root),[evidence]);
      this.db.atomic(()=>{
        this.db.put(old?this.db.nextVersion(old,{summary:fresh.summary,body:fresh.body,evidence:[evidence],status:'observed'}):fresh,'project');
        if(old)this.db.invalidateDependents(id);
        this.db.setMeta(key,revision);
      });created++;
    }
    const verify=this.db.locations(this.db.getMeta('verificationCursor'),Math.min(64,maxRecords));
    let invalidated=0;
    for(const location of verify.items){
      abort(signal);if(location.type==='remote'||!location.evidence.sourceAvailable)continue;
      try {this.evidenceRead(location,128);}catch{invalidated+=this.db.markUnavailable(location.evidence.id).length;}
    }
    this.db.setMeta('verificationCursor',verify.cursor??'');
    return {indexed:index,created,skipped,invalidated,cards:this.db.count(),cursor:page.nextCursor,complete:page.complete,
      projects:this.catalog.projects.map(path=>({path,scope:this.catalog.scope(path)})),modelCalls:0};
  }
  private correct(input:Input):unknown {
    const old=this.card(this.id(input));
    if(old.originDeviceId!==this.options.deviceId)throw new Error('ORIGIN_CORRECTION_REQUIRED');
    const body=text(input.body??input.correctedText,16384),summary=text(input.summary,256)||clip(body,256),reason=text(input.reason,1024);
    if(!body.trim()||!reason.trim())throw new Error('CORRECTION_AND_REASON_REQUIRED');
    const evidence=this.managedEvidence({type:'correction',ref:old.id,version:old.version,reason,body,at:Date.now()},'source_trace');
    return this.db.atomic(()=>{
      const card=this.db.nextVersion(old,{summary,body,status:'hypothesis',evidence:[evidence],lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()});
      this.db.put(card,this.db.kind(old.id));
      const invalidated=this.db.invalidateDependents(old.id);
      return {card,invalidated};
    });
  }
  private parents(input:Input):{cards:KnowledgeCard[];evidence:KnowledgeEvidence[]} {
    const refs=input.evidenceRefs??input.evidence;
    if(!Array.isArray(refs)||!refs.length||refs.length>32||refs.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'))throw new Error('EVIDENCE_REQUIRED');
    const cards=new Map<string,KnowledgeCard>(),evidence=new Map<string,KnowledgeEvidence>();
    for(const ref of refs as string[]){
      let card=this.db.get(ref);
      if(card){card=this.refresh(card);assertLive(card);cards.set(card.id,card);for(const e of card.evidence)evidence.set(e.id,e);continue;}
      const loc=this.db.evidence(ref);if(!loc)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_NOT_FOUND');
      if(!loc.evidence.sourceAvailable)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_NOT_CURRENT');
      this.evidenceRead(loc,128);
      for(const cardId of this.db.evidenceCards(ref)){
        const parent=this.refresh(this.card(cardId));assertLive(parent);cards.set(cardId,parent);
      }
      evidence.set(ref,loc.evidence);
    }
    if(!cards.size||evidence.size>32)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_REQUIRED');
    return {cards:[...cards.values()],evidence:[...evidence.values()]};
  }
  private async activate(card:KnowledgeCard,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<{card:KnowledgeCard;activated:boolean;reason:string}> {
    if(!this.options.validationRunner)return {card,activated:false,reason:'VALIDATION_POLICY_NOT_CONFIGURED'};
    abort(signal);
    const receipt=await this.options.validationRunner(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(card)) as KnowledgeCard,signal);
    abort(signal);
    if(!receipt||!text(receipt.executionId,256)||!text(receipt.validatorVersion,256)||!Number.isSafeInteger(receipt.exitCode)||
      typeof receipt.postconditionPassed!=='boolean')throw new Error('INVALID_VALIDATOR_RECEIPT');
    const evidence=this.managedEvidence({type:'validation',cardId:card.id,revision:card.contentHash,...receipt,at:Date.now()},'postcondition');
    if(receipt.exitCode!==0||!receipt.postconditionPassed)return {card,activated:false,reason:'VALIDATION_FAILED'};
    for(const parent of card.derivedFrom)assertLive(this.refresh(this.card(parent)));
    const next=this.db.nextVersion(card,{status:'validated',evidence:[...card.evidence,evidence],lastVerifiedAt:Date.now()});
    if(next.evidence.length>32)throw new Error('EVIDENCE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED');
    this.db.atomic(()=>this.db.put(next,'procedure'));
    return {card:next,activated:true,reason:'EXECUTED_VALIDATION_PASSED'};
  }
  private async learn(input:Input,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<unknown> {
    const parents=this.parents(input),scope=input.scope===undefined?parents.cards[0].scope:scopeOf(input.scope);
    for(const parent of parents.cards)if(parent.scope.visibility==='project'&&
      (scope.visibility!=='project'||scope.projectId!==parent.scope.projectId))throw new Error('SCOPE_WIDENING_REQUIRES_SEPARATE_EVIDENCE');
    const steps=input.steps;
    if(!Array.isArray(steps)||!steps.length||steps.length>20||steps.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||Buffer.byteLength(x)>1024))
      throw new Error('PROCEDURE_STEPS_REQUIRED');
    const summary=text(input.summary??input.title,256);if(!summary)throw new Error('SUMMARY_REQUIRED');
    const body=(steps as string[]).map((step,i)=>(i+1)+'. '+step).join('\n');
    const key=text(input.key,256)||hash(canonical({body,scope})),id='k:'+hash(this.options.deviceId+':procedure:'+key);
    const old=this.db.get(id);
    let card=this.newCard(id,summary,body,'hypothesis',scope,parents.evidence,parents.cards.map(c=>c.id));
    if(old) {
      const equivalent=old.body===body&&canonical(old.scope)===canonical(scope)&&
        canonical(old.evidence.filter(e=>e.kind!=='postcondition').map(e=>e.id).sort())===canonical(parents.evidence.map(e=>e.id).sort());
      if(equivalent&&old.status==='validated')return {card:old,activated:true,deduplicated:true,reason:'EXISTING_VALIDATION'};
      card=this.db.nextVersion(old,{summary,body,status:'hypothesis',scope,evidence:parents.evidence,derivedFrom:parents.cards.map(c=>c.id)});
    }
    this.db.atomic(()=>{this.db.put(card,'procedure');if(old)this.db.invalidateDependents(id);});
    if(input.autoActivate===false)return {card,activated:false,reason:'CANDIDATE_ONLY'};
    return this.activate(card,signal);
  }
  private async skills(input:Input,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<unknown> {
    const action=text(input.action,64)||'list';
    if(action==='read'){
      const card=this.refresh(this.card(this.id(input)));this.checkScope(card,input);
      if(this.db.kind(card.id)!=='procedure')throw new Error('SKILL_NOT_FOUND');
      if(card.status!=='validated'&&input.includeCandidates!==true)throw new Error('SKILL_NOT_ACTIVE');
      const response={card,body:card.body,active:card.status==='validated'};
      if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>boundedInt(input.maxBytes,65536,1024,65536))throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
      return response;
    }
    if(action==='rollback'){
      const id=this.id(input),old=this.db.get(id,boundedInt(input.version,0,1,Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER));
      if(!old||this.db.kind(id)!=='procedure'||old.status!=='validated')throw new Error('VALIDATED_REVISION_REQUIRED');
      const currentEvidence=new Set<string>();
      for(const parent of old.derivedFrom){const current=this.refresh(this.card(parent));assertLive(current);for(const e of current.evidence)currentEvidence.add(e.id);}
      if(old.evidence.some(e=>e.kind!=='postcondition'&&!currentEvidence.has(e.id)))throw new Error('CORRECTED_EVIDENCE_CANNOT_ROLLBACK');
      if(!this.options.validationRunner)throw new Error('VALIDATION_POLICY_NOT_CONFIGURED');
      const current=this.card(id),candidate=this.db.nextVersion(current,{...old,status:'hypothesis',version:current.version+1,
        evidence:old.evidence.filter(e=>e.kind!=='postcondition')});
      this.db.atomic(()=>this.db.put(candidate,'procedure'));
      return this.activate(candidate,signal);
    }
    if(action!=='list')throw new Error('SKILL_ACTION_UNSUPPORTED');
    const maxBytes=boundedInt(input.maxBytes,8192,512,65536);
    const response={...(this.search({...input,kind:'procedure',status:input.includeCandidates===true?undefined:'validated',
      includeInactive:false},true) as {items:KnowledgePreview[];truncated:boolean;nextCursor:string|null}),
      activationPolicy:this.options.validationRunner?'configured':'candidate_only'};
    while(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes&&response.items.length){
      response.items.pop();response.truncated=true;response.nextCursor=response.items.length?response.items[response.items.length-1].id:null;
    }
    if(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response),'utf8')>maxBytes)throw new Error('BUDGET_TOO_SMALL');
    return response;
  }
}
export function createPalaceProvider(options:PalaceOptions):PalaceProvider {return new Provider(options);}

import { isAbsolute } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import type { PluginContext, PluginInstance } from '../../platform/plugin-contract.js';
import { createPalaceProvider } from './index.js';
import type { PalaceOptions } from './types.js';

/** Distribution adapter only; the platform loads this through the generic plugin contract. */
export async function createPlugin(context:PluginContext):Promise<PluginInstance> {
  const settings=context.settings??{};
  let validationRunner:PalaceOptions['validationRunner'];
  if(settings.autoActivateSkills===true){
    if(typeof settings.validationModule!=='string'||!isAbsolute(settings.validationModule))
      throw new Error('TRUSTED_VALIDATION_MODULE_REQUIRED');
    const module=await import(pathToFileURL(settings.validationModule).href) as {
      createValidationRunner?:(context:PluginContext)=>PalaceOptions['validationRunner']|Promise<PalaceOptions['validationRunner']>;
    };
    if(typeof module.createValidationRunner!=='function')throw new Error('VALIDATION_FACTORY_REQUIRED');
    validationRunner=await module.createValidationRunner(context);
    if(typeof validationRunner!=='function')throw new Error('VALIDATION_RUNNER_REQUIRED');
  }
  const provider=createPalaceProvider({dataRoot:context.dataRoot,deviceId:context.deviceId,projectRoots:context.projectRoots,
    sourceRoots:context.sourceRoots,discoverKnownSources:settings.discoverKnownSources!==false,
    autoIndex:settings.autoIndex!==false,indexBudget:settings.indexBudget as PalaceOptions['indexBudget'],validationRunner});
  return {
    start:()=>provider.start(),stop:()=>provider.stop(),
    async invoke(capability,input,invocation){
      try{return await provider.invoke(capability,input,invocation);}
      catch(error){
        // Stable provider domain failures are call errors, not plugin process faults.
        if(error instanceof Error&&/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+(?::.*)?$/.test(error.message)){
          const coded=error as Error&{code?:string};coded.code=error.message.split(':')[0];throw coded;
        }
        throw error;
      }
    }
  };
}
export function migrate(options:{dataRoot:string;fromVersion:number;toVersion:number}):void {
  if(options.toVersion!==1||options.fromVersion>1)throw new Error('PALACE_MIGRATION_ADAPTER_REQUIRED');
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE src/plugins/palace/retrieval.ts | original-bytes=2719 | sha256=813f12c81bdc9bb54e1242f99165775da80d4912d93e69ef7a0aedf2562085ec =====
import type { KnowledgeCard } from './types.js';

// Query grammar contributes no evidence. A bounded lexical stage supplies candidates;
// the coverage floor prevents generic shared words from masquerading as an answer.
const STOP=new Set(('a an the and or of to for from in on at by with without after before while during as than '+
  'is are was were be been being do does did can could should would will may might must have has had '+
  'i my we our you your it its this that these those how why what which when where who '+
  'not just only also although but if then so need needed necessary another about '+
  'la cua va hay cho tu trong tren tai voi sau truoc khi nhu thi toi chung ban mot nhung cac nay do '+
  'can phai nen lam sao gi tai sao de khong').split(/\s+/));
const words=(value:string)=>(value.normalize('NFKD').replace(/\p{M}/gu,'').toLowerCase().match(/[\p{L}\p{N}_]+/gu)??[]);
export function queryTerms(query:string):string[] {
  const raw=[...new Set(words(query))],meaningful=raw.filter(term=>!STOP.has(term));
  return meaningful.length?meaningful:raw;
}
function stem(term:string):string {
  if(term.length<5||term.includes('_'))return term;
  if(term.endsWith('ies'))return term.slice(0,-3)+'y';
  if(term.endsWith('ing')&&term.length>6)return term.slice(0,-3);
  if(term.endsWith('ed')&&term.length>5)return term.slice(0,-2);
  if(term.endsWith('s')&&!term.endsWith('ss'))return term.slice(0,-1);
  return term;
}
export function rankCandidates(cards:KnowledgeCard[],query:string,limit:number):KnowledgeCard[] {
  const terms=[...new Set(queryTerms(query).map(stem))];
  if(!terms.length)return [];
  const sets=cards.map(card=>new Set(words(card.summary+' '+card.body).map(stem)));
  const frequencies=new Map(terms.map(term=>[term,sets.filter(set=>set.has(term)).length]));
  // FTS5/BM25 already bounds the candidate corpus. Long natural-language paraphrases
  // should not become harder to retrieve merely because they contain more unmatched words;
  // require two independent lexical signals, while short queries still require one.
  const minimum=terms.length>=3?2:1;
  return cards.map((card,index)=>{
    const matched=terms.filter(term=>sets[index].has(term));
    const information=matched.reduce((score,term)=>score+Math.log(1+(cards.length+1)/((frequencies.get(term)??0)+1)),0);
    return {card,matches:matched.length,score:information*(.5+matched.length/terms.length)};
  }).filter(row=>row.matches>=minimum)
    .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||statusRank(a.card)-statusRank(b.card)||a.card.id.localeCompare(b.card.id))
    .slice(0,limit).map(row=>row.card);
}
function statusRank(card:KnowledgeCard):number {return card.status==='validated'?0:card.status==='observed'?1:2;}

===== END SOURCE src/plugins/palace/retrieval.ts =====

===== BEGIN SOURCE src/plugins/palace/sources.ts | original-bytes=6198 | sha256=ace7db86e1e643b581d62d5b5719d2bc8e8ad0c60b6cee8ee55bf4fceb17e917 =====
import { existsSync, lstatSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename, join, parse as parsePath, relative, resolve, sep } from 'node:path';
import { homedir } from 'node:os';
import { execFile } from 'node:child_process';
import type { KnowledgeScope, PalaceOptions } from './types.js';
import { hash, clip } from './util.js';

export interface DocumentSource { path:string; scope:KnowledgeScope; kind:'document'|'skill'; }
const inside=(root:string,path:string)=>{const r=relative(root,path);return r===''||(!r.startsWith('..'+sep)&&r!=='..'&&!parsePath(r).root);};
export class SourceCatalog {
  readonly projects:string[];
  readonly sources:string[];
  readonly defaults:boolean;
  constructor(options:PalaceOptions) {
    this.projects=[...new Set((options.projectRoots??[]).map(p=>resolve(p)))].slice(0,32);
    this.defaults=options.discoverKnownSources??options.sourceRoots===undefined;
    const known=this.defaults?[
      join(homedir(),'.pi','agent','sessions'),join(homedir(),'.claude','projects'),
      join(homedir(),'.codex','sessions'),join(homedir(),'.mcp-palace')
    ]:[];
    this.sources=[...new Set([...known,...(options.sourceRoots??[])].map(p=>resolve(p)))].slice(0,32);
    for(const root of this.projects)for(const name of ['.pi','.claude','.codex','.mcp-palace'])
      this.sources.push(join(root,name));
    for(const root of [...this.sources,...this.projects])if(root===parsePath(root).root)throw new Error('FULL_DISK_SOURCE_FORBIDDEN');
  }
  scope(path:string):KnowledgeScope {
    const absolute=resolve(path),root=[...this.projects].sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length).find(r=>inside(r,absolute));
    if(!root)return {visibility:'account'};
    const name=basename(root),collision=this.projects.filter(p=>basename(p)===name).length>1;
    return {projectId:collision?name+'@'+hash(root).slice(0,8):name,visibility:'project'};
  }
  logRoots():string[] {return [...new Set(this.sources)].filter(p=>existsSync(p));}
  documents(maxFiles=32):DocumentSource[] {
    const out:DocumentSource[]=[];
    const add=(path:string,scope:KnowledgeScope,kind:'document'|'skill')=>{
      if(out.length>=maxFiles||!existsSync(path))return;
      try {const stat=lstatSync(path);if(stat.isFile()&&!stat.isSymbolicLink()&&stat.size<=256*1024)out.push({path,scope,kind});}catch{}
    };
    const skills=(dir:string,scope:KnowledgeScope)=>{
      if(!existsSync(dir)||out.length>=maxFiles)return;
      try{
        for(const entry of readdirSync(dir,{withFileTypes:true}).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).slice(0,32))
          if(entry.isDirectory()&&!entry.isSymbolicLink())add(join(dir,entry.name,'SKILL.md'),scope,'skill');
      }catch{}
    };
    for(const root of this.projects){
      const scope=this.scope(root);
      for(const name of ['README.md','AGENTS.md'])add(join(root,name),scope,'document');
      skills(join(root,'skills'),scope);
    }
    if(this.defaults)skills(join(homedir(),'.codex','skills'),{visibility:'account'});
    for(const source of this.sources)if(/\.(md|markdown)$/i.test(source))add(source,this.scope(source),'document');
    return out;
  }
}
export function readDocument(path:string):{bytes:Buffer;text:string;hash:string} {
  const st=statSync(path);if(!st.isFile()||st.size>256*1024)throw new Error('DOCUMENT_TOO_LARGE');
  const bytes=readFileSync(path);
  const text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(bytes);
  return {bytes,text,hash:hash(bytes)};
}
export function extractDocument(text:string):{summary:string;body:string} {
  const lines=text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(l=>l.trim());
  const title=(lines.find(l=>/^#{1,3}\s+\S/.test(l))??lines[0]??'Project document').replace(/^#+\s*/,'');
  const meaningful=lines.filter(l=>!/^---$|^<!--|^\|[\s:|-]+\|$/.test(l.trim()));
  return {summary:clip(title,256),body:clip(meaningful.join('\n'),8192)};
}
function extract(value:unknown):string {
  if(typeof value==='string')return value;
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.map(extract).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
  if(!value||typeof value!=='object')return '';
  const x=value as Record<string,unknown>;
  if(typeof x.text==='string')return x.text;
  if(x.content!==undefined)return extract(x.content);
  if(x.output!==undefined)return extract(x.output);
  return '';
}
export function extractObservation(raw:string,kind:string,tool:string):{summary:string;body:string;status:'observed'|'hypothesis'}|null {
  let text=raw;
  try{
    const x=JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string,unknown>;
    const m=x.message as Record<string,unknown>|undefined,p=x.payload as Record<string,unknown>|undefined;
    text=extract(m?.content??p?.output??p?.content??p?.message??x.text??x.output??x.content)||raw;
  }catch{}
  text=text.replace(/\u001b\[[0-9;]*m/g,'').trim();
  if(!text||['session','session_info','user','user_message'].includes(kind))return null;
  const lines=text.split(/\r?\n/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const informative=lines.filter(l=>! /^(?:ok|done|waiting|running|polling|success|exit code[: ]+0|\d+%)\.?$/i.test(l));
  if(!informative.length)return null;
  const joined=informative.join('\n');
  const isResult=/tool_result|tool_call_result/.test(kind);
  const explicit=/\b(?:decision|constraint|because|must|never|error|failed|exception|fix|solution|resolved|requires|unsupported|timeout)\b/i.test(joined);
  if(!isResult&&!explicit)return null;
  if(!isResult&&kind==='tool_call')return null;
  const salient=informative.find(l=>/\b(?:error|failed|exception|decision|constraint|fix|resolved|requires|unsupported|timeout)\b/i.test(l))??informative[0];
  const prefix=isResult?'Observed '+(tool||'tool')+' output: ':'Source claim: ';
  return {summary:clip(prefix+salient,256),body:clip(joined,8192),status:isResult?'observed':'hypothesis'};
}
export async function gitSnapshot(root:string):Promise<string|null> {
  if(!existsSync(join(root,'.git')))return null;
  return new Promise(resolveSnapshot=>{
    execFile('git',['-C',root,'log','-5','--format=%H%x09%ct%x09%s'],{timeout:2000,maxBuffer:32768,windowsHide:true},(error,stdout)=>{
      if(error){resolveSnapshot(null);return;}
      resolveSnapshot(String(stdout).trim()||null);
    });
  });
}

import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { chmodSync, copyFileSync, existsSync, lstatSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, readlinkSync, rmSync, statfsSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
